pattern-fix (record per IMPROVE THE MACHINE): this run's dependency audit table header read `| package | upstream deps |` while `gate_passes()` in `.github/workflows/opencode-schedule.yml` demands the exact string `| package | upstream deps found |`, and `tools/verify-fedora.sh` checked neither that header nor the `status: complete` line. So the verifier could report PASS while the workflow gate rejected the same block, which is what has been failing recent runs as "autonomous run failed" with a complete-looking relay. - GUI smoke correction (real finding): `protonplus --help` exits 1 headless AND under bare xvfb with "Unknown command '--help'" and a bare `protonplus` run SIGABRTs in a minimal container, because GTK4 needs libGLESv2.so.2 (mesa-libGLES) and a machine-id for the session bus. With `dnf install mesa-dri-drivers mesa-libGLES mesa-libEGL dbus-daemon` plus a generated /etc/machine-id, `timeout 20 xvfb-run -a protonplus` stays alive the whole window (GUI_ALIVE=yes, only Adwaita size warnings). Earlier "protonplus --help rc=0" claims were pipe-artifacts, not a package bug. The package is fine; the harness smoke was wrong.

harness-note: prompt Agent-pattern `dnf install ... spectool` is wrong (spectool is not a package, it ships in rpmdevtools); working set is `dnf-plugins-core rpm-build rpmdevtools forge-srpm-macros go-srpm-macros`. First-pass rpmbuild without `dnf builddep -y` fails with Failed build dependencies (harness artifact, not spec bug). AGENTS.md `golang-srpm-macros` name does not exist on F44; real name `go-srpm-macros` (provides %gosource).

Harness lesson relearned: container-teardown.sh takes the bare spec filename (it already cd's into the copied package dir). Dir-prefixed spec paths fail builddep with file-not-found. Early parallel failures this pass were that caller error, not spec bugs. Portal --help exits 1 with no session bus, so its smoke is an ldd link check. Protonplus takes a `version` subcommand, not --version. Heroic needs seccomp=unconfined plus non-root xvfb launch (rc=124 alive, zero setuid lines); plain docker gives a namespace artifact crash, which is not a packaging bug.
